### [150] [CausalForge: A Formally Grounded, Self-Improving Agentic Framework for Automated Research in Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22511)
*Jiyuan Tan,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CausalForge是一个基于Lean证明助手的自动化因果推断理论研究框架，结合形式化验证与声明审计，解决了LLM审稿人不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM审稿人可靠性差，无法有效区分真伪论文；自动化理论研究不仅需要生成候选结果，还需要可靠的评估机制。

Method: 构建CausalLean形式化因果推理库（7,035个机器检查的声明），开发CausalSmith自改进智能体流水线，结合内核验证与声明审计（对比形式定理与非正式声明）。

Result: 完成了自主研究运行的完整流程，产出了代码、形式化库和运行记录。

Conclusion: 机器检查的证明只能保证形式语句从假设中推导而来，无法保证语句忠实反映科学意图，因此需要额外的声明审计环节。

Abstract: Automating theoretical research is constrained not only by the generation of candidate results, but also by their reliable evaluation. A common approach is to close the research loop with a large language model (LLM) reviewer. However, such reviewers remain empirically unreliable: they may accept fabricated papers and detect them at rates close to chance (Bad Scientist, 2025). We present CausalForge, a framework for automated theoretical research in causal inference grounded in the Lean proof assistant. CausalForge combines Causalean, a foundational Lean library for causal inference containing 7,035 machine-checked declarations developed with language-model assistance under human design and review, with CausalSmith, a self-improving agentic pipeline that selects research topics, proposes results, formalizes statements, constructs proofs, and presents the resulting artifacts for human inspection. Because a machine-checked proof establishes only that a formal statement follows from its assumptions, not that the statement faithfully captures the intended scientific claim, the pipeline augments kernel verification with a statement audit that compares each formal theorem against the informal claim it is intended to express. We evaluate the system using artifacts produced by completed autonomous research runs. The source code, formal library, and run records are available at https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/CausalForge.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Influence of Electrohydrodynamic on Droplet Stability in Leaky Dielectric Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21638)
*Saurabh Sharma,Franck Lefebvre,Mostafa S Shadloo,Bertrand Lecordier*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了均匀电场中硅油液滴（悬浮于蓖麻油中）的形变动力学，包括变形、伸长、振荡和旋转。利用阴影成像和三维拉格朗日粒子追踪（3D-LPT/Shake-The-Box）技术，表征了液滴内部电流体动力学流动。发现液滴先呈扁球形，高电场下出现电致流体动力学不稳定性，轴与电场方向成角度，随后出现振荡变形并最终达到瞬态稳定构型。研究根据相对介电和流体性质，识别并表征了从低电场初始变形到高电场振荡行为的不同液滴行为区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解介电液滴在均匀电场下的变形动力学，特别是实验表征液滴的变形、伸长、振荡、旋转行为以及内部电流体动力学流动，识别不同电场强度下液滴行为的特征区域。

Method: 采用两相配置（硅油液滴悬浮于蓖麻油中），使用阴影成像技术观测液滴形变；利用荧光示踪粒子和基于Shake-The-Box（STB）技术的三维拉格朗日粒子追踪（3D-LPT），详细表征液滴内部的电流体动力学流动。

Result: 硅油液滴在蓖麻油中（S/R > 1）初始变形为扁球形状；足够高的电场强度下发生电致流体动力学不稳定性，液滴轴与电场方向成角度排列；进一步增加场强，液滴表现出振荡变形，随后进入瞬态稳定构型。

Conclusion: 研究成功识别并表征了从低电场初始变形到高电场振荡行为的多个不同液滴行为区域，这些行为取决于两相的相对介电和流体性质。

Abstract: This work examines the deformation dynamics of dielectric liquid droplet when exposed to a uniform electric field. The experimental investigation involves two-phase configurations, here as silicone oil droplets suspended in castor oil. The droplet dynamics including deformation, elongation, oscillation, and rotation are investigated over a range of electric field strengths using shadow-imaging. In parallel, fluorescent tracer particles were employed to perform threedimensional Lagrangian Particle Tracking (3D-LPT) using the Shake-The-Box (STB) technique, allowing for a detailed characterization of the internal electrohydrodynamic flow within the droplet subjected to a uniform electric field. For silicone oil droplets in castor oil medium (S/R > 1), the droplets initially deform into oblate shape. At sufficiently high electric field strengths, the droplet undergoes an Electrohydrodynamic instability, aligning their axis at an angle to the direction of electric field due to the imbalance in induce electric torque. Upon further increasing the field strength, the droplets display oscillatory deformation before settling into a transiently stable configuration. The current study identifies and characterizes the distinct regimes of droplet behaviour, from initial deformation at low electric fields to oscillatory behaviour at high field strengths depending upon the relative dielectric and fluid properties of the liquid phases.

</details>


### [152] [Explainable quantum-compressed machine learning for complex fluid flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21688)
*Xiao Xue,Maida Wang,Mingyang Gao,Minh Chung,Peter V. Coveney*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出量子压缩机器学习（QCML），将流动代理的潜传播器参数从524,288个压缩至最多8个，使学习到的动力学规律从黑箱神经网络变为物理本构关系级别的可解释、可控模型。通过结构化量子电路实现压缩，其酉传播器将潜频谱严格约束在单位圆上，将指数误差增长替换为线性累积。在心流通道和两个心血管基准上，QCML在保持预测精度的同时实现了长期稳定性和物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习代理面临可解释性与表达力之间的矛盾：可解释模型难以捕捉复杂非线性流动，而高表达力的深度代理通过大量参数化将学习到的动力学变成黑箱，缺乏可解释性和可控性。

Method: 提出QCML方法，利用结构化量子电路实现潜传播器的极端压缩（524,288→8参数）。该量子电路通过构造保证酉性，将潜频谱严格约束在单位圆上，从而将自回归推演中的误差增长从指数级降为线性级。共享相位和耦合角参数直接对应模态频率和模式间相互作用，赋予学习动力学物理意义。

Result: QCML在湍流通道流中保持完整推演稳定，而经典量子启发基线在一个李雅普诺夫时间内崩溃。在两个患者特异性心血管基准上，QCML在表面压力谱、压降和壁面剪应力等指标上达到与经典对应方同等的预测精度。

Conclusion: QCML解决了机器学习代理中可解释性与表达力的矛盾，以极端参数压缩实现了物理可解释、长期稳定且预测准确的流动代理，为科学机器学习提供了实用组件，并向实际预测中的量子优势迈出了具体一步。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogates of physical systems face a paradox: explainable models facing the challenge of expressivity to capture complex nonlinear flows, whereas expressive deep surrogates match high-fidelity simulations only through massive parameterisations that turn the learned dynamics into a black box. Here, we introduce quantum-compressed machine learning (QCML), which resolves this tension by compressing the latent propagator of a flow surrogate from $524{,}288$ trainable parameters to no more than $8$. This parameter reduction brings the learned dynamical law to the parameter scale of a physical constitutive relation rather than a black-box neural network, making the surrogate directly interpretable and controllable without sacrificing expressivity. The compression is realised by a structured quantum circuit whose unitary propagator constrains the latent spectrum to the unit circle exactly and by construction, replacing exponential error growth with linear accumulation over autoregressive rollouts. Classical regularisation only approximates this constraint: even a quantum-inspired classical baseline penalised towards unitarity collapses within one Lyapunov time on turbulent channel flow, whereas QCML remains stable over the full rollout. Shared phase and coupling angles parameterising the circuit correspond directly to modal frequencies and inter-mode interactions, giving the learned dynamics a physical interpretation in spectral space. On two patient-specific cardiovascular benchmarks, the structured QCML propagator matches the predictive accuracy of its classical counterpart on surface pressure spectra, pressure drop, and wall shear stress. These results establish QCML as a working component of scientific machine learning and a concrete contribution towards practical quantum advantage in real-world prediction.

</details>


### [153] [Load-dependent Taylor dispersion in a compliant electroosmotic pump conveying a simplified Phan-Thien-Tanner fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21791)
*Subhajyoti Sahoo,Ameeya Kumar Nayak*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文构建了无溶剂简化Phan-Thien-Tanner流体在柔性狭缝微通道中的电渗泵送与被动溶质色散的耦合模型，自洽评估了压力、壁面变形、轴向电场、速度和色散沿有限通量泵特性的变化，推导出闭式通量关系，并揭示了粘弹性、壁面柔顺性和双电层厚度对泵送与分离性能的复杂耦合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一个能够同时描述电渗泵送和被动溶质色散的耦合模型，考虑到粘弹性（sPTT流体）和壁面柔顺性，以理解这些因素如何影响微流控泵的传输和分离性能，并建立负载-分辨率关系以平衡压力输送与分离表现。

Method: 采用润滑理论近似、Debye-Hückel静电模型、弹性基础壁面定律和Taylor-Aris宏观输运理论，推导出结合电渗和压力驱动的闭式通量关系。通过电流守恒耦合轴向电场与变形间隙，分别考虑恒流和恒压操作模式。利用布朗动力学模拟验证简化模型。

Result: 通量随压力梯度单调递减，确保给定通量下唯一反演。无压流中，双电层变薄产生活塞流廓线，Newtonian Taylor系数与Debye参数平方成反比。有液压负载时，逆向压力梯度驱动剪切核心逆流，在薄双电层极限下系数趋于有限平台。有限通量下，电渗与压力驱动剪切的部分抵消在有限双电层厚度处产生最大塔板数。联合优化通量和双电层厚度时，最优向自由流、薄双电层操作偏移。粘弹性可增强或抑制负载色散，柔顺性改变泵特性和分离优化点。

Conclusion: 壁面柔顺性和粘弹性对微流控电渗泵的传输和分离特性有显著影响，通过联合优化通量和双电层厚度可实现最佳分离性能，所建立的负载-分辨率关系为平衡压力输送与分离性能提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We develop a coupled model for electroosmotic pumping and passive-solute dispersion of a solvent-free simplified Phan-Thien-Tanner fluid in a compliant slit microchannel. Pressure, wall deformation, axial field, velocity, and dispersion are evaluated self-consistently along the finite-throughput pump characteristic. Lubrication theory, Debye-Huckel electrostatics, an elastic-foundation wall law, and Taylor-Aris macrotransport yield a closed-form flux relation for combined electroosmotic and pressure-driven forcing. Because the shear rate depends cubically on the total shear stress, the two contributions cannot be superposed. The flux decreases monotonically with pressure gradient, ensuring a unique inversion at prescribed throughput. Current conservation couples the axial field to the deformed gap under constant-current and constant-voltage operation. In pressure-free flow, thinning the electric double layer produces a plug-like profile and the Newtonian Taylor coefficient decays as the inverse square of the Debye parameter. Under hydraulic loading, an adverse pressure gradient drives a sheared core counterflow that persists in the thin-double-layer limit, causing the coefficient to approach a finite plateau. At fixed nonzero throughput, partial cancellation between electroosmotic and pressure-driven shear yields a maximum plate number at finite double-layer thickness. This optimum is conditional: joint optimization over throughput and double-layer thickness shifts the overall optimum toward free-flow, thin-double-layer operation. Viscoelasticity can enhance or suppress loaded dispersion, while compliance shifts the pump characteristic and separation optimum. Brownian dynamics validates the reduced model. The resulting load-resolution relation identifies conditions that balance pressure delivery and separation performance.

</details>


### [154] [Flow Reversal in Low-Prandtl-Number Convection via Lateral Confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21984)
*Zhi-Han Wu,Long Chen,Yan-Wu Cao,Liang Xue,Ming-Zhu Ai,Juan-Cheng Yang,Ming-Jiu Ni*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过在Pr=0.029的液态金属对流中开展直接数值模拟，发现强侧向约束能够克服低普朗特数流体中高热扩散率对大尺度环流反转的抑制作用，机制在于约束诱导的"羽流凝聚"转变使混沌热羽流重组为准线性相干结构，降低热输运损耗，从而为角涡提供足够浮力驱动反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有共识——低Pr流体中LSC反转被抑制，探究侧向约束能否克服高热扩散率的稳定化效应。

Method: 对Pr=0.029的液态金属进行直接数值模拟，分析不同侧向约束条件下的流场结构、热耗散及参数空间特性。

Result: 发现强侧向约束可使混沌羽流重组为高度相干的准线性结构（"羽流凝聚"转变），该相干性大幅降低输运过程中的热损失，使羽流能为角涡贡献足够浮力以驱动反转；在参数空间中绘制出独特的"反转岛"区域。

Conclusion: 侧向约束可作为控制参数，克服高热扩散率的稳定化效应，开启低Pr流体中的LSC反转。

Abstract: A prevailing consensus holds that flow reversals of the large-scale circulation (LSC) are suppressed in low-Prandtl-number (Pr) fluids, as high thermal diffusivity rapidly dissipates the energy required to fuel the corner-vortex mechanisms. Here, we report Direct Numerical Simulations of liquid metal convection (Pr=0.029) revealing that strong lateral confinement defies this consensus, enabling sustained LSC reversals. We show that confinement triggers a ``plume condensation" transition, reorganizing chaotic thermal plumes into highly coherent, quasi-linear structures. A thermal dissipation analysis demonstrates that this coherence drastically reduces heat loss during transport, allowing plumes to deliver sufficient buoyancy to corner vortices to drive reversals. We map a distinct ``island of reversal" in the parameter space, establishing lateral confinement as a control parameter capable of overcoming the stabilizing effects of high thermal diffusivity.

</details>


### [155] [Study of discrete boundary layer suction for transition delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22051)
*Mukund R.,Pranav Joshi,Ishan Singh*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究了离散穿孔吸气对Blasius边界层转捩的影响，发现吸气体积流量是比吸气速度更优的延迟转捩指标，且存在最优吸气量可使转捩延迟最大化；较少的穿孔排数在略低的吸气体积流量下可实现稍大的转捩延迟，而在较低雷诺数下施加吸气可获得更显著的转捩延迟效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估离散穿孔吸气对Blasius边界层转捩的影响，并确定能最大程度延迟转捩的最优吸气参数（吸气强度、孔排数、雷诺数）。

Method: 使用热线风速仪测量流向速度脉动的间歇因子和频谱；在位移厚度雷诺数1400-1600范围内，采用4排或10排孔（孔径与边界层位移厚度同量级）进行吸气实验；通过改变吸气强度并辅以尺度分析来评估转捩延迟效果。

Result: 吸气强度增加时转捩前沿先向下游移动（最大延迟出现在最优吸气量），继续增加则转捩前沿回移；吸气体积流量比吸气速度更能表征吸气对转捩延迟的影响；相同体积流量下不同孔排数效果相似，但更少排数可实现稍大的转捩延迟和略低的最优吸气体积流量；较低雷诺数下吸气可显著增大转捩延迟，但归一化最优吸气体积流量略高。

Conclusion: 离散穿孔吸气可有效延迟Blasius边界层转捩，存在最优吸气量；吸气体积流量是更优的设计参数；减少孔排数及在较低雷诺数下施加吸气有利于增强转捩延迟效果。

Abstract: In the present study we have assessed the effect of suction through discrete perforations on the transition of the Blasius boundary layer. In particular, the intermittency and the spectra of the streamwise velocity fluctuations, obtained using hot-wire anemometry, were used to evaluate the optimum suction, i.e., one that provides the greatest delay in transition. The displacement thickness Reynolds number at suction was varied between 1400 and 1600, while the number of rows of holes was either four or ten. The suction holes had diameter of the order of the displacement thickness of the boundary layer, a practical size from manufacturing and maintenance perspectives. As the suction strength was increased the transition front moved downstream, with the maximum delay in transition occurring at the optimum suction. When the suction was increased further, the transition front moved upstream again. The results, supported by a scaling analysis, showed that suction volume flow rate is a better indicator of the effects of suction on the transition delay than suction velocity. In particular, suction through different numbers of rows of perforations produces largely similar effects on the boundary layer transition, provided the suction volume flow rate remains the same. However, suction through fewer rows provided a slightly greater delay in transition in addition to a slightly lower optimum suction volume flow rate. The results also showed that suction applied at a lower Reynolds number provides a substantially greater transition delay but at slightly higher values of the normalized optimum suction volume flow rate.

</details>


### [156] [Modeling and Stabilization of Transport-Dominated Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22455)
*Christopher Agesen,Sam Bradford,Abhijnan Dikshit,Anshi Gupta,Bhavana Morankar,Jack Murphy,Nanda N. Raghunathan,Karnav Raval,Lane H. Rogers,Valeria Barra*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文比较了大气输运主导流的数值稳定化方法，在Julia和Python平台上测试了SU/SUPG、超扩散和拟单调限制器等方法。拟单调限制器最有效抑制非物理极值但扩散结构，SUPG在保持锐利特征方面最佳，但未改善误差指标。提出了基于渐近分析的SUPG算法作为未来工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气建模中输运主导流存在数值振荡问题，需要有效的稳定化方法来抑制伪振荡同时保持流动的锐利特征。

Method: 在ClimaCore.jl和Firedrake两个框架中实现SU和SUPG稳定化方法，与超扩散和拟单调限制器方法对比，采用带槽圆柱初始条件的测试问题。

Result: 拟单调限制器最小化非物理极值效果最好但扩散整体结构；SUPG在保持锐利特征方面最佳；SUPG在计算误差指标上未优于无稳定化情况。

Conclusion: 不同稳定化方法各有优劣，拟单调限制器控制极值但牺牲结构保真度，SUPG保留特征但误差指标无改善，提出渐近SUPG算法作为未来方向。

Abstract: This report explores and compares numerical stabilization methods for transport-dominated flows arising in atmospheric modeling. The Streamline-Upwind (SU) and Streamline-Upwind Petrov-Galerkin (SUPG) stabilization formulations are implemented in Julia's ClimaCore.jl package and Python's Firedrake package for a test problem with slotted-cylinder initial conditions. These methods are compared for their ability to mitigate spurious oscillations while preserving sharp features against existing hyperdiffusion and quasi-monotone limiter methods, alongside various combinations. For this benchmark, quasi-monotone limiters were most effective at minimizing un-physical extrema, at the expense of diffusing the overall structure. The SUPG method was not found to improve upon the no stabilization case, for this test case in the computed error metrics. However, it performs best at preserving sharp feature and overall structure, among tested stabilized runs. Theoretical properties of SUPG are analyzed, and an asymptotics-based SUPG algorithm is proposed as future work.

</details>


### [157] [Numerical Validation of Lyapunov-Liouville Theory and Non-Diffusive Closures in Decaying Isotropic Fluid and Scalar Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22105)
*Nicola de Divitiis*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过自由衰减均匀各向同性湍流的数值模拟，系统验证了Lyapunov-Liouville理论框架及其非扩散湍流闭合模型的准确性，涵盖三种初始状态和宽普朗特数范围，成功捕捉了不同的衰减路径、标度律及间歇性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是对Lyapunov-Liouville理论框架及其非扩散湍流闭合模型进行全面的数值验证，检验其在自由衰减均匀各向同性湍流中的预测能力。

Method: 采用高精度自主架构，对von Karman-Howarth和Corrsin方程闭合系统进行数值积分，涵盖三种初始状态（Saffman-Birkhoff、Loitsiansky、高斯相关剖面）和Pr=10^-3至1000的宽范围，分析瞬态阶段、自保持扩散 regime及速度/温度增量概率密度函数。

Result: 闭合模型准确捕获了不同初始条件下的衰减路径：Saffman-Birkhoff渐近指数m=n=-1.25；Loitsiansky加速机械衰减(m=-1.51)但热持久性更高(n=-0.89)；高斯剖面导致超快衰减(m=-2.7)，Rλ在t=33时低于10。Pr=1000时热微尺度低于Kolmogorov尺度，Batchelor常数收敛于C_B=3.5，Obukhov-Corrsin常数匹配C_OC=1.8。PDFs捕捉到从低Pr准高斯统计到高Pr增强尺度依赖小尺度间歇性的转变。

Conclusion: 该理论框架在多尺度标量混合中具有预测鲁棒性，成功复现了Batchelor标度律及不同Pr数下的间歇性特征。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive numerical validation of the Lyapunov-Liouville theoretical framework and its non-diffusive turbulence closures under freely decaying homogeneous isotropic turbulence (HIT). The closed system of von Karman-Howarth and Corrsin equations is integrated via an autonomous, high-accuracy architecture across three initial states (Saffman-Birkhoff, Loitsiansky, and Gaussian correlation profiles) and Prandtl numbers from Pr = 10^-3 to 1000. The analysis scrutinizes the transient phase, the self-preserving diffusive regime, and the internal structure of turbulence via velocity and temperature increment probability density functions (PDFs). Our findings reveal that the closure accurately captures distinct decay paths. The Saffman-Birkhoff case yields asymptotic exponents m = -1.25 and n = -1.25. The Loitsiansky condition accelerates mechanical decay (m = -1.51) due to higher dissipation but exhibits higher thermal persistence (n = -0.89). Conversely, the Gaussian profile induces ultra-rapid decay (m = -2.7), reaching its operational limit at t = 33 as R_lambda drops below 10. Furthermore, the model replicates the non-equilibrium evolution of characteristic scales. At Pr = 1000, the thermal microscale drops below the Kolmogorov scale, confirming Batchelor's scaling where the Batchelor constant converges to C_B = 3.5 and the Obukhov-Corrsin constant matches C_OC = 1.8. Finally, the synthesized PDFs capture a sharp transition from quasi-Gaussian statistics at low Pr to enhanced, scale-dependent small-scale intermittency at high Pr, validating the predictive robustness of the theory for multi-scale scalar mixing.

</details>


### [158] [Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for Oscillatory Flows in Aeroelasticity with Application to Transonic Buffet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22402)
*Michael Candon,Pier Marzocca,Earl Dowell*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理引导的神经微分方程降阶模型，将非线性流体振荡器、有限记忆多输入Volterra级数和紧凑神经网络校正相结合，用于高效预测跨音速抖振诱发的气动弹性失稳，并在ONERA OAT15A翼型上验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自激气动流广泛存在于各类系统中，会驱动非线性流固耦合和气动弹性失稳，这类问题预测困难且计算成本高，因此需要开发高效的降阶模型。

Method: 提出物理引导的神经微分方程ROM：①非线性流体振荡器 + ②有限记忆多输入Volterra级数 + ③紧凑神经网络校正；多输入气动公式推广至m个结构模态，捕捉直接和非线性交叉模态耦合；通过单次预设运动CFD仿真（同步激励所有保留模态）识别模型，再与结构运动方程耦合进行气动弹性预测。

Result: 在ONERA OAT15A翼型跨音速抖振问题上，该ROM成功预测了气动弹性稳定性、频率锁定和极限环振幅，与全阶参考解吻合良好，并提供了对涉及多个结构模态的抖振诱发气动弹性失稳的新见解。

Conclusion: 该ROM能够高效且准确地预测跨音速抖振诱发的多模态气动弹性失稳，为理解复杂流固耦合现象提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Self-excited aerodynamic flows arise across a broad range of systems and can drive nonlinear fluid-structure interactions and aeroelastic instabilities that are challenging and computationally expensive to predict. This paper presents a physics-guided neural differential equation (DE) reduced order model (ROM) combining a nonlinear fluid oscillator, a finite-memory multi-input Volterra series, and a compact neural network correction. The multi-input aerodynamic formulation is generalized to m structural modes, capturing direct and nonlinear cross-modal coupling. The model is identified from a single prescribed-motion CFD simulation with simultaneous excitation of all retained structural modes, and is then coupled with the structural equations of motion for efficient aeroelastic prediction. Applied to transonic buffet over the ONERA OAT15A airfoil, the time-marching ROM predicts aeroelastic stability, frequency lock-in, and limit cycle amplitudes in good agreement with full-order reference solutions. The ROM is used to provide substantial new insight into buffet-induced aeroelastic instabilities involving more than one structural mode.

</details>


### [159] [Integral relations for the skin-friction coefficients and other quantities of annular flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22171)
*Pierre Ricco*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文推导了环形圆柱流中壁面摩擦系数与雷诺剪切应力之间的积分恒等式，分别给出了压力驱动和圆柱驱动情况下的关键关系式，并利用这些公式分析现有数据，在极端半径比下退化为经典管道/槽道流恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立环形圆柱流中壁面摩擦系数与雷诺剪切应力之间的积分关系，填补该几何构型下理论恒等式的空白。

Method: 通过数学推导得到积分恒等式，并利用现有数值和实验数据进行验证分析。

Result: 在压力驱动下得到最大流向速度径向位置的恒等式；在圆柱驱动下得到 bulk 速度的恒等式；在半径比趋于0或无穷时退化为经典槽道流和管道流结果。

Conclusion: 所推导的恒等式为环形圆柱流分析提供了理论工具，且与经典结果在极限情况下一致。

Abstract: Integral identities relating the skin-friction coefficients and the Reynolds shear stresses in annular cylindrical flows are derived. In the pressure-driven case, an identity for the radial location of the maximum streamwise velocity is obtained and, in the cylinder-driven case, an identity for the bulk velocity is found. The formulas are used to analyse existing numerical and experimental data and simplify to classical channel-flow and pipe-flow identities in the limiting cases of vanishingly small and infinitely large radius ratios.

</details>


### [160] [Bubble bursting in a sessile droplet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22207)
*U. J. Gutiérrez-Hernández,B. Muñoz-Sánchez,A. Agúndez-Cruz,J. M. Montanero,D. Fernández Rivas,M. G. Cabezas*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过实验和数值模拟研究了附着液滴中气泡破裂的动力学过程，发现液滴曲率和受限空间增强了能量聚焦，导致低粘度下产生更细更快的Worthington射流，高粘度下液滴喷出的Laplace数阈值低于无限液体环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解附着液滴中气泡破裂的物理机制，特别是液滴曲率和受限几何对能量聚焦和射流形成的影响，以及与传统无限液池中气泡破裂现象的差异。

Method: 实验观测与数值模拟相结合，系统分析不同粘度（Laplace数）下气泡破裂后射流和液滴喷射行为，重点关注液滴界面曲率引起的额外压力梯度作用。

Result: 1) 附着液滴曲率和受限空间显著增强能量聚焦；2) 低粘度下产生更细、更快的Worthington射流；3) 高粘度下液滴喷射的Laplace数阈值低于无限液池；4) 液滴界面曲率产生的额外压力梯度是驱动液体向空腔底部压缩、加速射流形成的关键机制。

Conclusion: 附着液滴的曲率和受限几何显著改变了气泡破裂动力学，通过增强能量聚焦降低了液滴喷射的临界条件，该效应与有限系统中粘性阻尼对短波振荡的抑制作用具有相似性。

Abstract: We analyzed experimentally and numerically the bursting of a bubble within a sessile droplet. Our experiments show that both sessile droplet curvature and confinement enhance the energy focusing. In the low-viscosity regime, this effect results in thinner, faster Worthington jets. In the high-viscosity regime, droplets are ejected for values of the Laplace number (the Reynolds number based on the visco-capillary velocity) smaller than the threshold for a bubble in an infinite liquid bath. This is probably the major result of the present work. Numerical simulations show the critical role of the additional pressure gradient arising from the curvature of the sessile droplet interface. The resulting force drives the liquid towards the bottom of the cavity, compressing it and accelerating jet formation. In the low-viscosity limit, the bottom of the cavity becomes smoother before jet ejection. This effect resembles the energy-focusing enhancement that occurs in an infinite liquid bath at the critical Laplace number, where short-wavelength waves are damped by viscosity.

</details>


### [161] [Critical assessment of RANS Models for Turbulent Heat Transfer in Low-Prandtl-Number Forced Convection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22246)
*Luca Marocco,Jonathan Schmitt,Jonathan Neuhauser,Bettina Frohnapfel,Davide Gatti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种湍流热输运模型在液态金属强制对流中的表现，发现k-ω SST模型配合Kays湍流普朗特数关联式最为稳健准确，四方程模型次之，而对数型四方程模型和代数热流模型则存在鲁棒性或可重复性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液态金属极低的Prandtl数（Pr）削弱了动量与热输运之间的相似性，使得基于雷诺比拟的传统RANS湍流热模型难以准确预测传热，需要系统评估现有热湍流封闭模型在低Pr流动中的表现。

Method: 基于OpenFOAM v2212，评估了五种湍流热输运组合模型：（1）k-ω SST模型配合Kays湍流Pr数关联式；（2）四方程k-ε-k_θ-ε_θ模型；（3）对数型k-Ω-k_θ-Ω_θ模型；（4）代数热流模型耦合低雷诺数k-ε模型；（5）代数热流模型耦合椭圆混合雷诺应力模型（EBRSM）。在通道流、管流和加热后向台阶流中进行验证，并与DNS数据和原文献结果对比。

Result: k-ω SST+Kays关联式在所有算例中表现最稳健、温度预测最准确；四方程模型可重复性好，与参考数据吻合满意，是最一致的多方程方案；对数型四方程模型数值鲁棒性降低；代数热流+k-ε模型在动量场预测正确的情况下无法复现已发表的热结果；EBRSM+代数热流能复现部分参考结果但鲁棒性严重不足。

Conclusion: 研究建立了所评估模型的统一表述形式，纠正了已发表版本中的不一致性，并验证了其可重复性和鲁棒性，为液态金属RANS热输运建模提供了可靠的模型选择指导。

Abstract: RANS modeling of turbulent heat transfer in liquid metals remains challenging because the very low Prandtl number weakens the similarity between momentum and thermal transport. Several thermal turbulence closures for forced convection in liquid metals are assessed using OpenFOAM v2212. The combinations include the $k$--$ω$ SST model with the Kays correlation for $\mathrm{Pr}_t$, the four-equation $k$--$ε$--$k_θ$--$ε_θ$ model, the logarithmic $k$--$Ω$--$k_θ$--$Ω_θ$ model, and two algebraic heat-flux formulations for $\overline{u_i'θ'}$ coupled with either a low-Reynolds $k$--$ε$ model or an elliptic blending Reynolds-stress model (EBRSM). They are evaluated in channel flow, pipe flow, and heated backward-facing step flow against DNS data and results from the original publications, focusing on reproducibility, robustness, and accuracy.
  Only a limited subset of models proves reliable for low-$\mathrm{Pr}$ flows. The $k$--$ω$ SST model with the Kays correlation gives the most robust performance and accurate temperature predictions in all cases. The $k$--$ε$--$k_θ$--$ε_θ$ model shows good reproducibility and satisfactory agreement with reference data, remaining the most consistent multi-equation alternative. The logarithmic four-equation model exhibits reduced numerical robustness, while the algebraic heat-flux model coupled with the $k$--$ε$ closure fails to reproduce published thermal results despite correct prediction of the momentum field. The EBRSM-based algebraic heat-flux formulation reproduces selected reference results but suffers from significant robustness limitations. The study establishes a unified formulation of the examined closures, correcting inconsistencies in their published forms, and verifies their reproducibility and robustness.

</details>


### [162] [Neptuna: A Comprehensive Machine Learning Framework for Benchmarking Complex Multiphase Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22280)
*Harish Ramachandran,Björn Kimpel,Thomas Paula,Josef Winter,Steffen Schmidt,Nikolaus Adams*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向激波驱动可压缩多相流的大规模基准测试（2.4TB 2D/3D数据），系统评估了多种机器学习代理模型（卷积、谱方法、Transformer、PDE基础模型）和复合损失函数（MSE+Sobolev/界面感知/结构感知）及自适应加权策略，发现单一模型无法在所有指标上最优，而复合损失能显著改善界面保持和谱保真度，SoftAdapt提供最稳定的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激波驱动的可压缩多相流（如气泡坍塌、液滴破碎）涉及激波、材料界面和强非线性耦合，现有的机器学习代理模型在此类问题上缺乏系统性的基准评估，亟需一个大规模、标准化的测试框架。

Method: 构建2.4TB的高保真2D/3D数据集（激波诱导气泡坍塌和液滴破碎），在Neptuna框架下评估卷积、谱方法、Transformer和PDE基础模型四类代理模型，对比标准MSE训练与复合损失（MSE+Sobolev、界面感知、结构感知）以及自适应损失平衡策略（SoftAdapt、GradNorm），采用点态、谱、特征聚焦、结构和物理感知等多维度指标。

Result: 无单一模型在所有数据集和指标上表现最佳；复合损失显著改善界面保持和谱保真度；SoftAdapt相比MSE单独训练提供最一致的提升且几乎无额外开销。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为压缩多相流代理模型提供了标准化评估平台，揭示了复合损失设计和自适应加权策略（特别是SoftAdapt）在复杂界面流动中的关键作用，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Compressible multiphase flows involving shocks and material interfaces arise in applications such as bubble collapse and droplet breakup, where strong nonlinear interactions produce complex interface deformation, mixing, and multiscale dynamics. Developing reliable machine learning surrogates for these flows remains challenging due to the simultaneous presence of compressibility, sharp discontinuities, and multiphase effects. In this work, we introduce the first large-scale benchmark specifically designed for shock-driven compressible multiphase flows, comprising 2.4 TB of high-fidelity 2D and 3D datasets \footnote{Dataset repo: https://huggingface.co/FluidVerse. Dataset sample videos, metadata.json, inference rollout plots from autoregressive rollout of the trained baselines are provided in the supplementary\_material.zip } featuring shock-induced bubble collapse and droplet breakup. We evaluate diverse surrogate model families on our benchmarking framework: Neptuna \footnote{Benchmarking repo: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/neptuna-A4E3}, including convolutional, spectral, transformer-based, and pre-trained PDE foundation models. Beyond standard MSE training, we investigate composite losses combining MSE with Sobolev, interface-aware, and structure-aware terms, together with adaptive loss balancing using SoftAdapt and GradNorm. Evaluation includes pointwise, spectral, feature-focused, structural, and physics-informed metrics. Results show that no single model performs best across all datasets and metrics, while composite losses significantly improve interface preservation and spectral fidelity. Among adaptive weighting strategies, SoftAdapt provides the most consistent improvements with almost no overhead compared to MSE-only training.

</details>


### [163] [PRIMS: Physics-guided Representation for Fluid Identification in Multimodal Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22422)
*Hai-Long Nguyen,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Lars Holm,Dennis Alveringh,Duc Viet Le*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: PRIMS是一种物理感知的多模态Transformer，通过将物理知识嵌入表示学习和注意力机制中，在动态流动、压力、温度条件下实现高精度、数据高效的流体识别，仅用0.46M参数即达到98.92% F1分数，模型规模减少14倍，并对未见工况具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的流体识别方法将传感器信号视为领域无关特征，忽略了支配流体行为的底层物理关系，导致泛化能力和可解释性受限。在动态流动、压力、温度条件下保持在线流体识别的可靠性仍是关键挑战。

Method: 提出PRIMS，一种物理感知多模态Transformer，包含三个专用模块：(1) 基于物理的令牌向量化，将原始科里奥利和压力传感器信号转换为物理有意义的令牌嵌入；(2) 物理组件合成器，建模流量、压力和密度之间与粘度相关的依赖性；(3) 物理引导融合，通过基于注意力的集成捕获跨物理相关性。

Result: 在五流体基准测试中，PRISM在动态流动、压力和温度条件下达到98.92%的平均F1分数，仅需0.46M参数，模型规模比现有最优Transformer方法减少14倍。在面向未见温度范围和未见流速范围的分布外偏移测试中，PRIMS持续优于此前最先进模型。

Conclusion: 明确镜像控制物理关系的架构设计能够学习可迁移、环境无关的表示，提升微流体传感在真实场景中的可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate on-device fluid identification is essential for microfluidic applications, yet maintaining reliability under varying flow, pressure, and temperature remains a key challenge. Existing learning-based methods often treat sensor signals as domain-agnostic features, neglecting the underlying physical relationships that govern fluid behavior, thereby limiting generalization and interpretability. To address this, we propose PRIMS, a physics-aware multimodal Transformer that integrates physical knowledge into representation learning and attention mechanisms through three dedicated modules: (1) Physics-based Token Vectorization transforms raw Coriolis and pressure sensor signals into physically meaningful token embeddings; (2) Physical Component Synthesizer models viscosity-related dependencies among flow, pressure, and density; and (3) Physics-guided Fusion captures cross-physical correlations through attention-based integration. By embedding these physics-based relationships directly into the model architecture, PRIMS bridges analytical fluid mechanics and deep learning, enabling interpretable, data-efficient, and resilient fluid classification. Evaluations on a five-fluid benchmark under dynamic flow, pressure, and temperature conditions show that PRIMS achieves 98.92% average F1-score with only 0.46 million parameters, a 14 times reduction compared to state-of-the-art Transformer-based methods. PRIMS also consistently outperforms prior SOTA models under out-of-distribution shifts to unseen temperature ranges and unseen flow-rate ranges, indicating strong robustness to operating conditions not observed during training. These findings suggest that designing architectures that explicitly mirror governing physical relationships can make them learn transferable, environment-independent representations, improving real-world reliability for microfluidic sensing.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [On the Removal of Solver-Induced Dependencies in Momentum-Weighted Interpolation for Primal and Continuous-Adjoint Flow Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21736)
*Niklas Kühl*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种修正的动量加权插值（MWI）方法，消除了求解器参数（松弛因子、时间步长）对压力和速度耦合的非物理依赖，确保原始离散格式不变的同时，在层流和湍流算例中获得与参数无关的一致结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MWI方法中，对角动量系数包含来自欠松弛和时间离散的贡献，导致目标量和伴随灵敏度非物理地依赖求解器参数，且当参数趋近于零时不存在明确定义的极限。

Method: 提出一种简单的修正方法，从压力驱动项的对角动量系数中移除求解器引入的贡献，以"配方"式步骤呈现，可推广到不同有限体积求解器。

Result: 二维层流圆柱绕流和三维湍流船体绕流中：未修正方法导致力、尾流量和形状灵敏度随求解器参数显著变化；修正方法在不同松弛因子和时间步长下结果一致。

Conclusion: 所提修正消除了参数的人为依赖，保持了原始离散格式，同时适用于原始和伴随系统，提高了CFD求解的鲁棒性和一致性。

Abstract: Momentum-Weighted Interpolation (MWI) is a key component in pressure--velocity coupling schemes on collocated cell-centered finite-volume methods for both primal and continuous adjoint formulations. In many practical implementations, MWI relies on diagonal momentum coefficients that include contributions from under-relaxation and time discretization. As a result, both primal quantities of interest and adjoint sensitivities may exhibit a non-physical dependence on solver parameters such as relaxation factors and time-step size, and no well-defined limit is obtained as these parameters approach zero.
  In this work, building on previous developments in discrete-consistent MWI formulations, a simple correction is proposed that removes solver-induced contributions from the diagonal momentum coefficients in the pressure-driven term. The resulting formulation preserves the original discretization while eliminating artificial dependencies on relaxation and time-stepping parameters and is applied consistently to both primal and adjoint systems. To facilitate its application, the derivation is presented in a structured, recipe-like manner that can be readily followed and transferred to different finite volume-based solver configurations.
  The proposed modification is assessed for a two-dimensional laminar cylinder flow and a three-dimensional turbulent ship hull flow configuration. In both cases, the uncorrected formulation leads to significant variations in forces, wake-related quantities, and shape sensitivities when solver parameters are altered, despite all simulations being iterated to converged residual levels and stable integral quantities. In contrast, the corrected formulation yields consistent results across a wide range of relaxation factors and time-step sizes.

</details>


### [165] [Learning Lattice Parameters from Powder X-Ray Diffraction Data Using Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21829)
*Elyssa Hofgard,Kyucheol Min,Nofit Segal,David W. Mittan-Moreau,Aria Mansouri Tehrani,Vanessa Oklejas,Jigyasa Nigam,Daniel W. Paley,Aaron S. Brewster,Tess Smidt*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双谱不变表示的机器学习方法，用于从粉末XRD数据预测晶胞参数，相比直接预测晶格参数，长度MAPE从11.18%降至2.44%，角度MAPE从12.74%降至3.07%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的从XRD数据直接预测晶格参数（a,b,c,α,β,γ）的方法依赖于晶胞归约或约定，导致预测质量受数据表示方式影响大。需要一种与晶胞约定无关的不变表示方法来提高预测准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了倒易晶格的双谱不变表示，该表示基于球谐投影的晶格点描述符，与原始胞约定无关。双谱计算是可微的，并采用动态规划方法进行反演。使用该双谱作为机器学习目标而非直接预测晶胞参数。

Result: 在MP-20数据集上，使用双谱表示相比直接预测，长度MAPE从11.18%降至2.44%，角度MAPE从12.74%降至3.07%。在实验RRUFF数据集上也进行了评估，并与Crystalyze等现有方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 基于双谱的不变晶格表示能显著提升XRD晶胞参数预测精度，且有望推广到其他晶体学机器学习任务（如结构生成）中作为几何感知目标。

Abstract: We present a machine learning (ML) method to determine unit cell parameters from powder X-Ray diffraction (XRD) data using a novel invariant lattice representation. In ML, the data representation used can have a substantial impact on the prediction quality. Previous approaches have directly predicted lattice parameters ($a,b,c,α,β,γ$) from XRD inputs. However, these parameters depend strongly on the unit cell reduction or convention used. In this work, we construct an invariant representation of the reciprocal lattice that is independent of primitive cell convention, based on the bispectrum--a descriptor built from spherical harmonic projections of lattice points. The calculation of the lattice bispectrum is differentiable, and we demonstrate how to invert it using a dynamic programming approach. We show that when fixing ML model architecture, using the lattice bispectrum as the ML target rather than the unit cell parameters leads to more accurate lattice parameter predictions. For example, using the MP-20 dataset, the bispectrum reduces length mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) from 11.18% to 2.44% and angle MAPE from 12.74% to 3.07% compared to direct prediction with the same model architecture. We additionally benchmark our approach against pre-existing XRD to crystal structure models such as Crystalyze and assess its performance on the experimental RRUFF dataset. Beyond unit cell representation, we anticipate this invariant lattice representation could serve more broadly as a geometry-aware target for other crystallographic machine learning tasks such as structure generation.

</details>


### [166] [Producing a complete nuclear data library for adjoint Monte Carlo simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22192)
*Paul Rovel,Coline Larmier,Davide Mancusi,Andrea Zoia*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用方法，能够处理现代中子数据评估中各种核反应的多样性，并创建完整的伴随核数据库，为在TRIPOLI-5蒙特卡罗代码中实现伴随抽样方案奠定基础。通过连续能量基准验证，该方法与标准正向蒙特卡罗模拟结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在反应堆辐射屏蔽应用中，源区域（堆芯）远大于探测器区域（剂量计），标准正向蒙特卡罗模拟效率较低。通过求解伴随输运方程可显著提高效率，但需要制备伴随核数据。现有方法难以处理现代中子数据评估中多样化的核反应类型，因此需要一种通用的伴随核数据库制备方法。

Method: 提出一种通用方法，能够处理现代中子数据评估中各类核反应的多样性，制备完整的伴随核数据库，为在TRIPOLI-5（CEA和ASNR开发的新一代蒙特卡罗代码）中实现伴随抽样方案奠定基础。

Result: 基于涉及重核和轻核混合物的连续能量基准配置进行验证，结果与标准正向蒙特卡罗模拟一致。

Conclusion: 该方法成功创建了完整的伴随核数据库，并在基准测试中得到了验证，为在TRIPOLI-5中实现伴随抽样方案迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: Radiation shielding applications related to reactor design typically involve situations where the source region (the core) is much larger than the detector region (a dosimeter). In such cases, the efficiency of Monte Carlo simulation might be significantly increased by solving the adjoint transport equation: adjoint particles are born from the detector, undergo adjoint ('reversed') flights and collisions, and accumulate their tallies in the source region. A key prerequisite to sample the adjoint collision events is the preparation of adjoint nuclear data. In this work, we propose a general method able to handle the diversity of nuclear reactions available in modern neutron data evaluations, and ultimately create a full adjoint nuclear data library. This is a stepping stone in view of implementing adjoint sampling schemes in TRIPOLI-5 __ , the next-generation Monte Carlo code developed by CEA and ASNR. We validate this strategy based on a relevant continuous energy benchmark configuration involving mixtures of heavy and light nuclides, and we compare our results to those obtained by standard forward Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [167] [Hyperspatial Sampling: Circumventing Free-Energy Barriers via Replica Exchange with Extra Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22417)
*Henrik Christiansen,Matheus Ferraz,Takashi Maruyama,Francesco Alesiani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出超空间副本交换（HS-REX）方法，通过在物理系统中添加额外空间维度绕过自由能势垒，仅对溶质原子施加惩罚以减少副本数，在双阱模型和丙氨酸二肽（显式水溶剂）中验证了比温度副本交换更优的遍历采样能力，包括手性构象的采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟具有粗糙自由能景观的体系仍是计算物理和化学的核心挑战，现有增强采样方法（如温度副本交换）对于溶剂化体系需要大量副本，计算成本高。

Method: 提出超空间副本交换（HS-REX），将物理系统通过附加空间维度扩展，利用高维空间中几何上不可达的路径绕过自由能势垒，且仅对溶质原子施加惩罚以大幅减少副本数量。

Result: 在双阱模型和显式水溶剂中丙氨酸二肽上验证：HS-REX不仅增强了对慢速骨架二面角的采样，还成功采样了标准三维采样无法达到的两种手性构象。

Conclusion: HS-REX相比传统温度副本交换实现了更优的遍历采样能力，对生物体系应用尤其有效。

Abstract: Simulating systems with rugged free-energy landscapes remains a central challenge in computational physics and chemistry. We introduce hyperspatial replica exchange (HS-REX), an enhanced sampling method in which the physical system is artificially extended by additional spatial dimensions. In higher dimensions, free-energy barriers can be circumvented through paths that are geometrically inaccessible in the original space. Restricting the penalty to only solute atoms dramatically reduces the number of replicas required for solvated systems compared to standard temperature replica exchange, a feature especially relevant for biological applications. As proof of concept, we demonstrate the method on a double-well model system and on alanine dipeptide in explicit water as benchmark system. In the latter case, HS-REX achieves enhanced conformational sampling of not only the slow backbone dihedral angles, but also both chiral configurations of the molecule, which are sterically inaccessible to standard sampling in three dimensions. This demonstrates enhanced ergodic sampling over conventional temperature replica exchange.
